To address these limitations, we propose CoG (Cognition on Graph), a cognitive-inspired, training-free framework for adaptive knowledge exploration. Drawing inspiration from human problem-solving, CoG performs a continuous plan-explore-reflect cycle, where it proactively formulates investigation plans, performs dual-source retrieval, and dynamically reflects on progress to adjust strategies. Crucially, it establishes deep bidirectional synergy between structured graph and unstructured text, where entities extracted from text dynamically guide graph exploration to bridge knowledge gaps. Extensive experiments on seven multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that CoG significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods while achieving superior exploration efficiency. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/zhougengxian/CoG.

</details>


### [106] [DuplexDrama: A Synthesized Dialogue Dataset with Scenarios, Full-Duplex Behaviors, Expressive Speech, and Sound Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12872)
*Qingxiang Guo,Wenke Fan,Shuofeng Zhao,Dawei Yang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Hongwei Cai,Zhou Wang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DuplexDrama，首个同时覆盖人设/场景、三种全双工行为、情感表达与音效事件的口语对话数据集，超2000小时，将开源6400段双语对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语对话数据集难以同时满足全双工行为、人设场景、情感表达和音效等多维度需求，制约全双工对话模型研究。

Method: 采用4阶段构建流水线，覆盖完整人设与场景、三种全双工行为（打断、反馈、不完整）、人设对齐情感标注的表达性语音及脚本感知音效事件，并对脚本与合成音频进行质量验证。

Result: 生成超2000小时音频，64种音色覆盖13类人设、5个年龄层；3.8%话轮含至少一种全双工行为；经内部全双工模型训练验证有效；将开源6400段双语对话（约800小时，中文约500h、英文约300h）及LLM评判评估提示。

Conclusion: DuplexDrama为全双工口语对话模型研究提供了高质量、多维度的大规模数据资源。

Abstract: We present DuplexDrama, the first synthesized spoken dialogue dataset that simultaneously covers four dimensions: (i) complete persona and scenario settings; (ii) three full-duplex behaviors (interruption, backchannel, incomplete); (iii) expressive speech with persona-aligned emotion labels; and (iv) script-aware sound events. DuplexDrama is built via a 4-stage pipeline; quality validation on both scripts and synthesized audio confirms its quality. We have produced more than 2,000 hours audio data with a 64-voice timbre pool spanning 13 personas and 5 age buckets; 3.8% of all turns carry at least one full-duplex behavior. This data has been validated through internal full-duplex model training. We will release a curated subset of 6,400 bilingual dialogues (800 h, Chinese ~500 h + English ~300 h) to advance full-duplex spoken dialogue model research. Data samples are available at our demo page and LLM-judge evaluation prompts will be released with the dataset.

</details>


### [107] [Parameter-Efficient Retrievers for Polish and European Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12913)
*Sławomir Dadas,Rafał Poświata,Małgorzata Grębowiec,Michał Perełkiewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出三阶段训练管线，无需人工标注，用大模型教师蒸馏出紧凑高效的多语言检索器PolDense和EuroDense。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流稠密检索依赖数十亿参数大模型，内存与算力开销大，难以支持大规模索引、频繁语料更新和低延迟服务。

Method: 三阶段训练管线：跨语言对齐、关系型知识蒸馏、对比微调；无需原始真值相关性标签，仅用强embedding模型与重排序器作为教师生成监督信号。支持8192 token上下文。

Result: PolDense：6个波兰语检索器（17M–1B）；EuroDense：435M，支持9种欧洲语言。在41个波兰语及150个多语言任务评测中，PolDense-1B超过所评测的最多9B参数模型，家族构成Pareto前沿；1B以下模型中EuroDense任务平均与语言平均均第一，9种语言中7种领先。

Conclusion: 验证了通过蒸馏驱动管线可构建高效紧凑检索器，实现强质量-效率权衡，并公开全部模型。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems increasingly rely on multi-billion-parameter language models, whose memory and computational requirements make large-scale indexing, frequent corpus updates, and low-latency serving costly. We present a three-stage training pipeline for developing compact and efficient retrievers that remain competitive with substantially larger models. The pipeline combines cross-lingual alignment, relational knowledge distillation, and contrastive fine-tuning. It requires no original ground-truth relevance labels, relying exclusively on supervision generated by strong embedding models and rerankers utilised as teachers. Using this pipeline, we develop PolDense and EuroDense, both supporting contexts of up to 8,192 tokens. PolDense is a family of six Polish retrievers ranging from 17M to 1B parameters. EuroDense is a 435M-parameter retriever supporting nine European languages. We conduct an extensive evaluation covering 41 Polish and 150 multilingual retrieval tasks. The results demonstrate strong quality-efficiency trade-offs. PolDense-1B outperforms the evaluated retrievers with up to 9B parameters, while the PolDense family forms the Pareto frontier across model sizes. Among the evaluated models below 1B parameters, EuroDense ranks first in both task-averaged and language-averaged performance and leads in seven of nine languages. We release all models publicly.

</details>


### [108] [LLM-Enhanced Dual-Branch Learning for Large-Scale Multi-Label Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12915)
*Hui Ye,Jing Zhang,Xiulong Yang,Rajshekhar Sunderraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DualMLC双分支框架，融合自回归解码器模型与双向编码器，通过晚期logit融合提升大规模多标签文本分类性能，在三个基准上达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表示的方法多以单一主编码器为中心，或在单一排序器中融合辅助特征，未能充分挖掘异构语言模型间的互补性。

Method: DualMLC将同一文档分别输入自回归解码器语言模型和双向编码器，两个分支各自维护表示路径，独立在共享标签空间上估计相关性得分，最后通过晚期logit融合组合两个得分向量。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的大规模多标签文本分类基准上取得SOTA；消融实验验证异构预测器融合比单一分支排名更强。

Conclusion: 异构语言模型分支的融合有效提升多标签分类排序效果，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-label text classification assigns a small subset of relevant labels to each document from a vocabulary containing thousands or tens of thousands of candidate labels. Although pretrained language models have improved semantic text representations, most representation-based approaches center their prediction pipelines on a primary encoder or combine auxiliary features within a single ranker. The complementarity between heterogeneous language models therefore remains insufficiently explored. We propose DualMLC, a dual-branch framework that processes the same document through an autoregressive decoder-only language model and a bidirectional encoder. Each branch maintains its own representation pathway and independently estimates relevance scores over the shared label space. DualMLC combines the two score vectors through late logit fusion, allowing shared evidence to reinforce relevant labels and branch-specific evidence to compensate for limitations in the other branch's representation. DualMLC achieves state-of-the-art results on three widely used large-scale multi-label text classification benchmarks. Ablation results further confirm that integrating the heterogeneous predictors produces stronger rankings than either branch alone. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/huiyegit/DualMLC.

</details>


### [109] [Fewer Words, Not Fewer Tokens: Measuring the Sanskrit Tokenization Penalty per Proposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12960)
*Devansh Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文考察梵语的高信息密度在子词分词后是否保留：按"命题/语义单位"而非按词统计，部署分词器下梵语相较英语耗费更多token，但差距随词表增大而缩小，稳健结论是梵语在当代散文与FLORES上约消耗英语1.831–2.899倍token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梵语通过词尾融合格、数、人称、时态并以复合词连缀从句，使单位词信息量很高，但这一密度是否能在子词分词（tokenization）中存续尚属未知；且该问题应在"每个语义单位"层面而非"每个词"层面评估。

Method: 在FLORES-200 devtest相同内容上比较梵语、英语、印地语的token数；用梵语侧训练的BPE与英语侧训练的匹配对照（相同算法、相同语料、词表32k/64k/128k）比较每命题token成本；并将比值分解为字符长度比与每字符token比。

Result: 部署分词器（≥200,019ids）下梵语为英语token的1.774–2.187倍，仅为印地语的1.325–1.353倍；对部署英语分词器，梵语训练BPE在当代散文上每命题比英语更便宜（0.887），但与匹配英语对照相比该翻转消失（32k/64k下8对全部>1.0且95%区间不含1.0）；128k时域内0.983、域外1.025、FLORES 1.116；比值主要由字符长度比驱动，每字符token比接近1，梵语散文SLP1长度比1.028、诗体0.596。

Conclusion: 稳健的结论关乎部署实践：在当代散文与FLORES上，以SLP1编码的梵语对应部署o200k英语枢轴，每命题耗费英语1.831–2.899倍token；所谓梵语分词更"便宜"的假象源于与部署英语分词器对比而非匹配对照，本质上残留的是字符级长度差异而非信息密度。

Abstract: Sanskrit fuses case, number, person and tense into word endings and chains clauses into compounds, so it is information-dense per word. Whether that density survives subword tokenization is a separate question, to be asked per unit of meaning rather than per word. On identical FLORES-200 devtest content, Sanskrit costs 1.774-2.187 times the English tokens under deployed tokenizers with vocabularies of 200,019 ids or more, but only 1.325-1.353 times the Hindi tokens. Against a deployed English tokenizer, Sanskrit-trained BPE arms then look cheaper per proposition than English on contemporary prose (0.887). Against a matched English control, the same algorithm and vocabulary trained on the English side of the same corpus, that flip disappears: at 32,000 and 64,000 pieces all 8 matched pairs, each size-matched arm against both a pair-matched and a byte-matched control, sit above 1.0 on prose with 95% intervals excluding it. The gap closes as the vocabulary grows: at 128,000 pieces the BPE pair reads 0.983 in domain while staying above parity out of domain (1.025) and on FLORES (1.116). The ratio factorises into a character-length ratio and a tokens-per-character ratio, the second near 1 throughout: what survives matched tokenization is character-level length, which Sanskrit prose lacks over English in SLP1 (1.028) and Sanskrit verse has (0.596). The robust statement is about deployed practice: on contemporary prose and on FLORES, with the Sanskrit side in SLP1 against the deployed o200k English pivot, Sanskrit costs 1.831-2.899 English tokens per proposition under the tokenizers people actually ship. Code, the results snapshot and every table here are public.

</details>


### [110] [Judging by the Cover: Cleaning LLM Truthfulness Benchmarks to Avoid Surface-Level Feature Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13003)
*Foad Namjoo,Remy Ogasawara,Amirali Abdullah,Cullen Anderson,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 二选一真值基准中，正确与错误答案存在表面特征差异，模型可据此作弊。本文提出检测与清洗机制 Audit-Prune。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二选一真值基准假设模型需推理才能判断对错，但若正确答案与错误答案在表面特征上系统不同，模型仅凭表面线索即可超随机水平，使评测失效。

Method: 用六特征逻辑回归分类器检测表面特征泄漏；验证其他基准中也存在类似问题；提出 Audit-Prune 机制，通过移除最强泄漏配对来清洗数据。

Result: 在 TruthfulQA 上，简单六特征分类器即可显著区分正确与错误答案；多个基准均存在类似表面伪影。

Conclusion: 表面特征泄漏可被检测并利用；发布清洗后 TruthfulQA（泄漏降至接近随机水平），并提供 Audit-Prune 供发布前清洗数据集。

Abstract: Binary-choice truth benchmarks ask models to choose between a correct and an incorrect answer, but if the two answers differ systematically in surface-level features, models can exceed chance without performing the intended reasoning. We show that this failure mode is detectable and can be exploited by downstream classifiers. In TruthfulQA, a simple six-feature logistic classifier achieves substantial accuracy in separating correct from incorrect answers. We further show that similar surface-level artifacts are present in additional benchmarks. To counteract this, we developed a general mechanism to clean them by removing the most leakage-reinforcing pairs. We release a version of TruthfulQA with surface-feature leakage reduced close to chance and provide a mechanism, Audit-Prune, so that the datasets can be cleaned before release.

</details>


### [111] [Tasks over Application Manuals: Revealing Gaps in Long-Horizon Procedural Reasoning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13005)
*Utkarsh Soni,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Sachin Chandrasekhar,Eugene Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TAM 基准，用于评估大模型在长程、规则驱动的程序性推理任务上的能力，发现现有方法表现极差（ICD-10-CM 1%、量刑 15.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多跳推理基准通常是短程的，仅需少量检索或推理步骤，无法反映真实世界中需要遵循数百页、复杂且相互依赖指南的可靠性问题。

Method: 构建 Tasks over Application Manuals (TAM) 基准，从 ICD-10-CM 临床编码和美国联邦量刑两个领域策展真实任务并经人工验证标注。每个任务需遵循含数万条规则的权威手册，跨章节执行一系列相互依赖的步骤以得到精确答案。评估了检索增强生成、ReAct 式提示及 agent-harness 基线（基于 GPT-5）。

Result: 最佳精确匹配表现极低：ICD-10-CM 编码仅 1%，量刑任务 15.5%。

Conclusion: 当前基准可能高估了 LLM 的推理能力，并忽视了关键挑战——可靠地遵循冗长、基于规则的程序。TAM 数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on a wide range of natural language tasks, and recent benchmarks suggest that they are increasingly adept at multi-hop reasoning. However, these benchmarks are typically short-horizon, requiring only a small number of retrieval or inference steps, and provide limited evidence of reliability on real-world tasks that involve following manuals spanning hundreds of pages with complex, interdependent guidelines. In this paper, we introduce Tasks over Application Manuals (TAM), a benchmark for evaluating long-horizon procedural reasoning. We construct TAM by curating real-world tasks from two domains: ICD-10-CM clinical coding (mapping medical conditions to diagnostic codes) and U.S. federal sentencing (computing crime sentencing guideline outcomes, specifically offense levels), with human-validated labels. Each task requires following an authoritative manual with tens of thousands of rules and executing a sequence of interdependent steps across different sections to produce an exact answer. We evaluate general-purpose prompting approaches, including retrieval-augmented generation, ReAct-style prompting, and an agent-harness baseline on GPT-5, and find that the best exact-match performance remains extremely low: 1% on ICD-10-CM coding and 15.5% on sentencing tasks. These results show that current benchmarks may overestimate LLM reasoning ability and miss a key challenge: reliably following long, rule-based procedures. The complete TAM data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [112] [Kraken: LLM-based Speech-to-Speech Translation via Low-bitrate VQ and Dual-path Source Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13045)
*Hayato Futami,Hassan Shahmohammadi,Tushar Dhyani,Alkis Koudounas,Raphaël Lafargue,Yosuke Kashiwagi,Quentin Jodelet,Emiru Tsunoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Kraken：基于Qwen3-8B的语音到语音翻译模型，采用单层矢量量化的低比特率token与源语音条件化的Autowave-X声码器，提升翻译质量与非语言信息迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音LLM做S2ST存在两个问题：(1) LLM难以预测高比特率语音token；(2) 依赖说话人身份与韵律对齐良好的S2ST训练数据。

Method: 用基于单层矢量量化、以重建自监督(SSL)特征为目标的低比特率token；采用独立的token到波形解码器Autowave-X，并以源语音为条件以改善非语言信息迁移，从而放宽数据约束；在Qwen3-8B上扩展语音特征输入与低比特率token输出，接Autowave-X声码器；使用15万小时多语言多任务语音数据训练。

Result: 翻译质量优于SeamlessM4T-Large v2与Qwen2.5-Omni，且说话人与韵律迁移能力提升。

Conclusion: 结合低比特率token与源语音条件化的声码器，可有效提升S2ST的翻译质量与非语言信息保留。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) has advanced significantly with speech LLMs, offering the potential for joint optimization and preserving non-linguistic information. However, these models struggle with predicting high-bitrate speech tokens in LLMs, and face the challenge of relying on S2ST training data with ideally aligned speaker identity and prosody. We propose using low-bitrate tokens based on single-layer vector quantization, trained to reconstruct self-supervised learning (SSL) features. We also employ a separate token-to-waveform decoder named Autowave-X, which is also conditioned on the source speech to improve non-linguistic transfer, thereby relaxing the training data constraints. With the integration of these techniques, we propose an S2ST model named Kraken, which augments a pre-trained LLM with speech feature inputs and the low-bitrate token outputs, followed by Autowave-X vocoder. We built the model upon Qwen3-8B and trained it using 150k hours of multilingual and multitask speech data. We demonstrated that our model exhibited better translation quality than SeamlessM4T-Large v2 and Qwen2.5-Omni, along with improved speaker and prosody transfer capabilities.

</details>


### [113] [Expert-Space Exploration in MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13058)
*Hongyi He,Zhenghao Lin,Xiao Liu,Peng Cheng,Yan Lu,Yeyun Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ESRL，一种面向MoE模型的架构感知强化学习框架，通过显式探索专家路由空间来增加rollout多样性，在多个骨干网络和任务上超越GRPO且无额外采样或计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE强化学习研究主要关注优化稳定性和训练效率，将专家选择视为固定组件。作者发现扰动专家路由能改变模型输出、增加rollout多样性（类似提高解码温度），但直接扰动会激活不合适的专家、严重降低rollout质量。

Method: ESRL保留高置信度专家作为锚点，将随机路由限制在合理的候选池内；根据router熵自适应调整扰动强度以避免过度扰动；记录rollout使用的专家路径并在策略优化时重放，缓解扰动带来的路由不匹配。

Result: 在top-K、top-1和共享专家路由等多种MoE骨干，以及数学、科学、代码任务上均取得最佳性能，且无额外采样或计算开销。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，平均Pass@1和Pass@8相比GRPO分别提升3.2和4.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 利用MoE特有的路由结构可有效提升RL训练效果，专家利用率和训练动态分析揭示了其内在机制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training of large language models. Recent advances in RL for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have primarily focused on improving optimization stability and training efficiency, while treating the expert selection as a fixed component. Since routing determines the sparse computation paths that induce output distributions, expert selection offers an additional source of rollout diversity. Through empirical analysis, we find that perturbing expert routing effectively alters model output and increases rollout diversity, which is similar to increasing the decoding temperature. However, direct perturbation can activate unsuitable experts and substantially degrade rollout quality. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Expert-Space Exploration Reinforcement Learning (ESRL), an architecture-aware framework that explicitly explores the expert-routing space of MoE models. ESRL preserves high-confidence experts as anchors, and restricts stochastic routing to a plausible candidate pool, thereby retaining reliable computation paths. The perturbation strength is further adapted according to router entropy to avoid over-perturbation. To mitigate the routing mismatch introduced by perturbation, ESRL records the expert paths used during rollout and replays them during policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that ESRL achieves the best performance across MoE backbones with top-K, top-1, and shared-expert routing, as well as across mathematics, science, and code tasks without additional sampling or computational cost. Specifically, ESRL on Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves the best among all compared methods, improving average Pass@1 and Pass@8 over GRPO by 3.2 and 4.5 percentage points, respectively. Further analyses of expert utilization and training dynamics provide insights into how exploiting MoE-specific routing structure benefits RL training.

</details>


### [114] [Continue, Adapt, or Yield: In-Turn Adaptation to Overlapping Speech in Full-Duplex Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13117)
*Yunqi Lu,Tyler Baumgartner,Nikhil Johri,Brandon Tai,Candice Fan,Luc Debaupte,Ruben Aguilar,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Duplex Cue 评测框架，用于衡量全双工语音代理在说话中途吸收听者插话内容的"轮内适应"能力，并以 PersonaPlex 做案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工评测只关注代理"继续说还是停下"这一二元判断，无法表达人类常用的第三种反应：一边继续说、一边吸收听者贡献的内容（补充词、纠正或澄清）。因此需要新的评测维度来刻画自然的语音交互。

Method: Duplex Cue 将听者意图（反向信道、协作、打断）与说话者行为（不变继续、轮内适应、让出）分开。适应既包含确认也包含内容修改。使用 300 条经人工确认、来自无脚本英语对话的线索，比较真人录音回应与在回放听者音频时生成的 PersonaPlex 续说，保留 208 对有可评分回应的配对。

Result: 在 66 对协作型配对中，真人说话者 68.2% 出现适应，而 PersonaPlex 仅 34.8%；模型其余情况为不变继续（42.4%）或让出（22.7%）。

Conclusion: 评测自然语音交互不仅需要衡量代理是否继续说话，还必须衡量它如何回应听者的贡献，Duplex Cue 为这一方向提供了可操作的评测方式。

Abstract: Full-duplex evaluation often emphasizes whether an agent keeps speaking or stops. That binary cannot express a third response humans use routinely: continuing to speak while incorporating what the listener just contributed. The contribution may be a missing word, a correction or a clarification. We introduce Duplex Cue, an evaluation of this \emph{in-turn adaptation} in full-duplex voice agents. Duplex Cue separates listener intent (backchannel, collaboration, or interruption) from speaker behavior: continuing unchanged, adapting within the turn, or yielding. Adaptation includes acknowledgment as well as content revision. In a single-model case study using 300 human-confirmed cues from unscripted English conversations, we compare recorded human responses with PersonaPlex continuations generated while replaying the listener's audio. We retain 208 pairs with the ongoing speaker active at cue onset and a scorable response in each condition. On the 66 collaborative pairs, recorded speakers adapt in 68.2\% of cases, compared with 34.8\% for PersonaPlex. The model otherwise continues unchanged (42.4\%) or yields (22.7\%). These findings show why evaluating natural voice interaction requires measuring how an agent responds to a listener's contribution as well as whether it keeps speaking.

</details>


### [115] [SAS: Simple Attention Sparsification via End-to-End Optimization of Context Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13141)
*Zhiwei Li,Lei Zhu,Hao Gu,Xiang Hu,Yan Wang,Haitao Mi,Sirui Han,Leo Liang,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAS（Simple Attention Sparsification），一种门控稀疏注意力机制，用语言建模损失端到端优化上下文排序，在大幅降低注意力成本的同时优于现有稀疏注意力基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可训练稀疏注意力方法使用轻量选择器打分后进行硬Top-K选择，导致梯度被阻断，只能通过蒸馏逐层稠密注意力分布来训练。这种排序与原模型在固定注意力预算下对预测的实际影响不一致，会浪费有限的注意力预算。需要一种让排序与下游预测直接对齐的机制。

Method: SAS将选择器的连续分数以对数的形式注入注意力softmax内部的logits，使语言建模损失可通过标准反向传播更新选择器，实现端到端优化。关键设计包括：门控置于softmax内部的对数形式；使用归一化softmax门以校准历史上下文与始终保留的当前块；保留连续选择器分数以学习相对优先级而非仅硬选择。并实现内存高效的Triton kernel集成到FlashAttention风格计算中。

Result: 在推理、长上下文理解和智能体任务上，SAS在不同注意力预算下持续优于可训练稀疏注意力基线，在紧张预算下提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过让选择器与语言建模损失端到端对齐，SAS实现了更有效的上下文排序，在多个下游任务上验证了该方法在稀疏注意力场景下的优越性。

Abstract: Post-training attention sparsification reduces the quadratic cumulative attention cost of pretrained Transformers by selecting a small set of context units (tokens or blocks) for each query. Existing trainable methods usually use a lightweight selector to score context units, followed by hard Top-K selection that blocks gradients from the language modeling loss. Consequently, these methods commonly distill layer-wise dense attention distributions. Although this encourages the selector to rank context units by dense attention weights in the original model, the ranking is not directly aligned with their impact on predictions under a fixed attention budget (i.e., the number of attended context units per query), potentially wasting the limited budget on less useful units. To address this misalignment, we propose Simple Attention Sparsification (SAS), a gated sparse attention mechanism that optimizes context ranking end-to-end with the language modeling loss. The key idea is to inject the selector's continuous scores into attention logits during training, allowing the loss to update the selector through standard backpropagation. We identify several choices crucial for this simple design to work well in practice: placing the gate inside the attention softmax in log form, using normalized softmax gates to calibrate historical context against the always-retained current block, and preserving continuous selector scores so the model learns relative priorities rather than only hard selections. To support long-sequence training, we implement a memory-efficient Triton kernel that integrates SAS into FlashAttention-style computation. Across reasoning, long-context understanding, and agentic tasks, SAS consistently outperforms trainable sparse attention baselines across attention budgets, with especially large gains under tight budgets, demonstrating more effective context ranking for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [116] [Type Diversity Enables Transformers to Generalise Compositionally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13144)
*Anssi Moisio,Mathias Creutz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文主张Transformer结构性泛化更难并非固有特性，而是源于数据集中词法类型多样性高、结构类型多样性低。作者通过Grammatical Framework构建COGS和SLOG的语言学多样化变体，发现类型多样性与组合泛化在两个层面上同等相关，支持其假设，并与"复合差异解释难度"的观点相矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将组合泛化分为词法泛化与结构泛化，并发现Transformer在结构泛化上更难。作者质疑这一难度差异是否为Transformer固有属性，认为它可能只是由特定数据集造成的——即词法类型多样、结构类型单一（高词法类型多样性、低结构类型多样性）。因此需要控制并变化类型多样性来检验这一混淆因素。

Method: 1) 用类型多样性（type diversity）定义：某类型不同constructor的数量，而非具体词组合。2) 在已有数据集（COGS、SLOG）上变化词法与结构类型的多样性。3) 使用Grammatical Framework（GF）创建语言学多样化的COGS与SLOG变体。4) 考察其他数据集属性（非新型测试结构的其他类型多样性、逻辑语义格式的表面属性）对组合泛化的影响。

Result: 类型多样性与组合泛化在词法测试用例和结构测试用例中具有同等程度的相关性，支持作者假设：结构泛化更难并非Transformer固有，而是数据集类型多样性不平衡所致。同时发现与先前工作"复合差异（compound divergence）解释组合泛化难度"的结论相矛盾。

Conclusion: 结构泛化比词法泛化更难，主要源于数据集类型多样性的差异，而非Transformer的固有局限。通过在COGS/SLOG中控制类型多样性可缩小两者差距。复合差异并非组合泛化难度的充分解释，且其他数据集属性（其他类型多样性、语义表面格式）也会影响组合泛化性能。

Abstract: Compositional generalisation has been divided into lexical and structural generalisation. Previous work has found that structural generalisation is harder than lexical for Transformers. We propose that this difference is not inherent to Transformers, but due to the high diversity of lexical types and low diversity of structural types in the specific datasets of these previous works. By type diversity we mean the number of different constructors of that type, instead of, for example, the specific word combinations that might populate the structure. To test this, we vary the amounts of type diversity of lexical and structural types in previously published datasets. We create linguistically diverse variants of the COGS and SLOG datasets using Grammatical Framework. We find that type diversity correlates with compositional generalisation equally in lexical and structural test cases, supporting our hypothesis. We note a contradiction with the proposition in previous work that compound divergence explains the difficulty in compositional generalisation tasks. We further investigate the effects of other dataset properties on compositional generalisation, such as the diversity of types other than the novel test structure, and surface properties of the logical semantics format.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Test-Driven Approaches to Software Engineering with Large Language Models: A Survey of Phases, Tasks, and Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12012)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述87篇文献，提出以"测试改变了什么决策"为核心的组织框架，区分TDD循环与测试条件生成、执行引导精炼、测试媒介分析、仅评估测试五类用法，并跨任务比较测试在LLM与软件工程代理中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试日益参与大语言模型和软件工程代理的决策（指定行为、指导构建修复、选择候选、约束变换、提供执行证据），其用法虽源于测试驱动开发，但在测试顺序、预言可得性、可编辑产物和执行角色上差异显著，缺乏统一定位与跨任务比较，因此需要结构化梳理。

Method: 结构化范围综述：整合87条研究与支撑记录，对83条做方法/协议级抽取，另收集5项实践资源；以"测试改变哪个决策"为组织问题，区分Red-Green-Refactor循环与测试条件生成、执行引导精炼、测试媒介分析、仅评估测试，并跨代码生成、修复、翻译、重构、克隆检测、代码搜索、定位、训练数据构建、形式规范验证等任务比较，另设专节分析代理工作流与可复用技能如何编码测试过程及其评估方式。

Result: 证据支持将测试可得性、测试有效性、反馈使用和评估独立性视为彼此独立的属性；仅测试通过并不能证明行为等价、有效反馈或流程遵循；总体改进也可能掩盖不同模型、任务和分母下的差异。研究将以上区分综合为机制分类法、跨任务比较与协议敏感的证据分析。

Conclusion: 本文提炼出机制分类法、跨任务比较和协议敏感的证据分析框架，并指出预言验证、因果评估、长周期维护和可复用测试驱动代理能力等未来研究方向。

Abstract: Tests increasingly participate in the decisions made by large language models and software engineering agents. They specify intended behavior, guide program construction and repair, select candidates, constrain transformations, and provide execution evidence for software analysis. These uses draw on test-driven development, yet differ substantially in test order, oracle availability, editable artifacts, and the role of execution. We present a structured scoping survey organized around the question of what decision a test changes. The review integrates 87 research and supporting records, with method- or protocol-level extraction for 83 records, alongside a separate collection of five practice resources. We distinguish the Red--Green--Refactor cycle from test-conditioned generation, execution-guided refinement, test-mediated analysis, and evaluation-only testing. We then compare code generation, repair, translation, refactoring, clone detection, code search, localization, training-data construction, and formal-specification validation. A dedicated analysis examines how agent workflows and reusable skills encode testing procedures and how their effects are evaluated. Across these tasks, the evidence supports treating test availability, test validity, feedback use, and evaluation independence as separate properties. Test passing alone does not establish behavioral equivalence, effective feedback, or process adherence; aggregate improvements can also conceal different outcomes across models, tasks, and denominators. We synthesize these distinctions into a mechanism taxonomy, a cross-task comparison, and a protocol-sensitive evidence analysis, and identify research directions in oracle validation, causal evaluation, long-horizon maintenance, and reusable test-driven agent capabilities

</details>


### [118] [When Agent Metrics Measure Different Things: An Evidence-Grounded Audit of the Praxa AI Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12017)
*Stefan G. Creadore,Peyton Woakz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这篇论文是对 Praxa AI 实现文件、历史评测产物与运行记录进行的回顾性"测量审计"，揭示评测数值虽正确但可能偏离标签所称构念的问题（如离线路由139例中112通过/27失败却零门控失败，因已知缺口被豁免；8443条工具尝试时长中121条等于32位有符号整数最大值2147483647ms，实际是生命周期截断伪值；单轨迹压缩试点报告94.39%降幅但触发+后续两次调用合计仅46.54%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评测常出现"数值正确但测的是另一构念"的伪有效问题：门控策略豁免、生命周期时长截断、请求级会计口径等会污染看似严谨的指标，导致对整体能力的过度推断，因此需要一份可复用的测量审计以区分门控策略、生命周期计时与请求级核算。

Method: 采用回顾性测量审计：检查 Praxa AI 实现文件、历史评测产物与运行记录；复现描述性计算；用独立的加权有理算术实现校验91项计时统计；运行13个评分函数与12个分析验证器测试；用有限完成边界量化缺失时长的影响而不做插补。

Result: 离线路由139例（112通过/27失败）因豁免而为零门控失败；标识符无关导出含8843行工具尝试，其中8395条有时长、448条缺失；121条时长等于32位最大值并带"废弃客户端"标签，数据库代码对生命周期年龄做钳制；池化99分位为2147483647ms，而服务器观测的完整调用为38118.31ms；压缩试点后续调用降幅94.39%，触发加后续合计降幅46.54%。

Conclusion: 贡献是一份带源链接的案例研究与可复用验证包，用于把门控策略、生命周期计时、请求级核算与更广泛的能力主张区分开；未独立复现历史与当前完整流水线，因此不确立普遍能力优越性与群体级统计显著性。

Abstract: Agent evaluations can be numerically correct while measuring a different construct from the one implied by their labels. We present a retrospective measurement audit of selected Praxa AI implementation files, historical evaluation artifacts, and operational records. A 139-case offline routing report contains 112 passes and 27 failures despite zero gating failures, because known gaps are explicitly exempted from the gate. An identifier-free export represents 8,843 tool-attempt rows: 8,395 recorded durations and 448 missing values. Of the durations, 121 equal the signed 32-bit maximum and carry abandoned-client labels; inspected database code clamps elapsed lifecycle age. The pooled recorded 99th percentile is 2,147,483,647 ms, versus 38,118.31 ms among server-observed completed calls. This is a stratum contrast, not a treatment effect. In a documented single-trajectory compaction pilot, the reported follow-up input reduction is 94.39%, but the reduction across the trigger and follow-up calls together is 46.54%. We reproduce the descriptive calculations, verify 91 timing statistics through a separate weighted rational-arithmetic implementation, and execute 13 scoring-function and 12 analysis-verifier tests. Finite-completion bounds show how missing durations limit all-row timing statements without imputing values. The contribution is a source-linked case study and reusable verification package for separating gate policy, lifecycle timing, and request-level accounting from broader agent-performance claims. Historical provider runs and the full current pipeline were not independently reproduced; general capability superiority and population-level statistical significance are not established.

</details>


### [119] [Missing Dimensions: Integrating Human and Social Systems into Digital Twin Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12131)
*Francis Bordeleau,Mark van den Brand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文主张数字孪生工程须明确识别四个系统维度（工程、自然/生物、人类、社会），并指出随维度向人类与社会延伸，重心应从自动控制转向决策支持与用户赋能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数字孪生的工程实践过于狭隘，仅聚焦于工程系统和自然系统，忽视了人类与社会维度及其带来的建模要求与伦理义务。

Method: 以智能建筑为贯穿案例，论证四维度各自的特性、建模需求与伦理义务，并提出论点。

Result: 提出四维度的数字孪生工程框架，并识别出多维数字孪生工程的四个开放研究挑战。

Conclusion: 数字孪生需纳入人类与社会维度，其目标应从自动控制转向决策支持与居住者赋能。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) have emerged as a key technology at the core of digital transformation, yet their engineering practice remains too narrowly focused on engineered and natural systems. This paper argues that four system dimensions must be explicitly recognized in DT engineering: Engineered, Natural/Biological, Human, and Social. Each dimension brings distinct properties, modeling requirements, and ethical obligations that fundamentally shape what a DT must represent and how it must be built. We further argue that as DTs extend into the Human and Social dimensions, the emphasis should shift from automated control toward decision support and occupant empowerment. We illustrate our arguments using a smart building as a running example and identify four open research challenges for multi-dimensional DT engineering.

</details>


### [120] [A decision-basis contract for auditable LLM-assisted medical billing verification: deterministic rules, verbatim evidence, and fail-closed abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12156)
*Jan Hölter,Kevin Geis,Benjamin Raab,Boris Bauke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"决策依据契约"的可审计LLM医疗账单核验概念验证，将确定性费用目录规则检查与LLM文本评估分离，通过强制证据和失败关闭机制保证可审查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗账单核验需要可审计性，但LLM判断缺乏可追溯依据，规则检查与语义评估混杂，难以验证结论是否正确。

Method: 构建决策依据契约：确定性层解析适用目录版本并检查编码可用性、数量限制与排除项；语义层将申报项分类为支持、矛盾或信息缺失，支持与矛盾须提供逐字证据片段；规则缺失、评估失败或缺证据时失败关闭式弃权。在合成目录与36个案例上评测4个本地开源模型，并做无明确文档要求的消融及端到端基线对比。

Result: 各模型结果一致性不一，无一致优于基线；明确文档要求提升了4个模型对信息缺失的识别；证据门控暴露了原始判断正确但缺有效证据、被转为不完整决策依据的案例。

Conclusion: 显式决策记录可使规则发现、文档判断与弃权原因可检查；实际价值仍需真实目录、独立标注文档及人工评审验证。

Abstract: This work presents a proof of concept for auditable LLM-assisted medical billing verification based on a decision-basis contract. The contract separates deterministic checks of versioned fee-catalog rules from LLM-based assessment of free-text documentation. The deterministic layer resolves the applicable catalog release and checks code availability, quantity limits, and exclusions. The semantic layer classifies each claimed item as supported, contradicted, or missing required information. Support and contradiction require a verbatim evidence span; unavailable rule context, unsuccessful assessment, or missing required evidence prevents support through fail-closed abstention. We evaluated four locally run open-weight models on a synthetic catalog and 36 curated cases under the contract, an ablation without explicit documentation requirements, and an end-to-end baseline. Outcome agreement varied across models and showed no consistent advantage over the baseline. Explicit documentation requirements improved identification of missing information for all four models. The evidence gate also exposed cases in which correct raw judgments lacked valid evidence and were converted to incomplete decision-basis entries. The results show how explicit decision records can make rule findings, documentation judgments, and abstention reasons inspectable. Evaluation on real catalogs, independently annotated documentation, and with human reviewers is required to assess practical value.

</details>


### [121] [Open Source Stewardship Communities: "We need you, but not your pull request"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12236)
*Gregorio Robles,Daniel M. German*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"守护型社区"(stewardship community)概念：AI降低实现成本，但审查他人贡献仍昂贵，导致部分开源项目限制外部实现贡献，核心维护者保留实现权，社区仅参与非编码工作。指出以人为中心软件工程不仅要保持人控制AI，还须关注AI替代实现劳动如何削弱开源社区的自我更新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI降低开源贡献的实现成本，但审查成本相对高昂，使项目开始限制外部实现贡献。这引发对人类社区可持续性的担忧：当编码智能体让维护者可替代过去由外部贡献者提供的实现工作时，人类社区将发生什么？

Method: 以概念性、观察性论述为主，提出并界定"守护型社区"这一形式，分析开源项目在AI时代对贡献访问权限与实现权归属的结构性变化。

Result: 识别出现象：一些项目限制实现贡献的准入，并非因代码由AI生成，而是因不再值得付出审查成本；实现权集中于小型核心，获得编码权限日益依赖审批而非自发贡献。

Conclusion: 对以人为中心的软件工程而言，仅让人类保持对AI智能体的控制是不够的：AI可替代实现劳动，同时削弱开源社区的自我更新机制。

Abstract: Human-centric AI for software engineering means keeping humans responsible for work performed with AI. In Open Source Software (OSS), AI lowers the cost of implementing changes, but reviewing someone else's contribution remains comparatively expensive, so some projects now restrict who may contribute implementations while still welcoming other participation---not because the code is AI-generated, but because it no longer justifies the review cost. We call the resulting form a stewardship community: a small core retains implementation authority while a broader community continues to shape the software without writing code, and access to coding increasingly depends on approval rather than self-initiated contribution. This raises a broader question: what happens to the human community when coding agents let maintainers replace implementation work once supplied by external contributors? For human-centric software engineering, keeping humans in control of AI agents is not enough: AI can replace implementation labor while weakening how OSS communities renew themselves.

</details>


### [122] [PQLS: A High-Performance Python Library for Steady-State Simulation of Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12309)
*Evan Simanovskis,Raviraj Adve,Javane Rostampoor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PQLS是一个基于JAX/XLA的高性能Python库，用于批量求解Lindblad主方程的稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模、多维参数扫描（如频谱、场响应、多普勒平均）中逐点求解稳态效率低，需要批量并行计算与硬件加速。

Method: 提供三层API（物理描述、模型参数、直接Hamiltonian与collapse算符）；用JAX将多个Hamiltonian表示为批张量，经JAX编译求解器向量化处理，减少Python开销。

Result: 在CPU与GPU上与QuTiP、QuTiP-JAX、RydIQule对比，测试配置下取得显著加速。

Conclusion: PQLS兼顾物理便利与数值控制，适合大规模参数扫描的量子稳态计算。

Abstract: PQLS (Parallel Quantum Liouvillian Solver) is a high-performance Python library for computing steady-state solutions of the Lindblad master equation. It provides a layered user-facing API with three levels of abstraction. The high-level API accepts physical descriptions of atomic ladder systems and automatically determines the required model parameters; the mid- level API accepts quantities such as Rabi frequencies, detunings, decay rates, and network topology; finally the low-level API allows users to directly specify the Hamiltonian and collapse operators. This layered design enables users to choose between physical convenience and direct numerical control depending on the application. PQLS is built on JAX and XLA to enable vectorized and hardware-accelerated computation of large parameter sweeps. Rather than solving the steady-state problem sequentially for each parameter configuration, PQLS represents multiple Hamiltonians as batched tensors and processes them through a JAX-compiled solver, reducing Python-level overhead and improving hardware utilization. This is particularly useful for applications requiring large or multidimensional parameter sweeps, such as computing frequency spectra, evaluating field-dependent responses, and performing Doppler averaging over atomic velocity distributions. PQLS has been benchmarked against QuTiP, QuTiP-JAX, and RydIQule on CPU and GPU platforms, demonstrating substantial computational speedups across the tested configurations.

</details>


### [123] [Hieronym: Leveraging Hierarchical Multi-Source Information for Function Renaming in Stripped Binary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12457)
*Xiaoling Zhang,Jian Sun,Dawei Wang,Chongyu Wang,Li Chen,Zhaoteng Yan,Peipei Liu,Lixiao Zhang,Dan Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Hieronym 是一个基于生成式大语言模型的剥离二进制函数重命名框架，通过层次化摘要驱动的领域适应与多源信息融合，显著超越现有方法并具强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剥离二进制中的函数重命名能提升代码可读性、辅助逆向工程，但从底层二进制代码中准确捕获跨指令集、架构与优化级别的函数语义，并用简洁可读名称表达，十分困难。现有方法或语义捕获不全面，或对未见二进制泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出 Hieronym，采用层次化摘要驱动的领域适应策略，并融合多源信息（全局二进制上下文、局部调用上下文、函数内在语义）来增强 LLM 对二进制代码的理解。同时提出含 token 级与整名级指标的双层评估框架。

Result: 在四种优化级别（O0-O3）与四种架构（x64、x86、ARM、MIPS）上评估，Hieronym 显著优于现有方法：token 级精确率提升 50.12%、召回率 41.75%、F1 提升 45.10%，名称级准确率提升 79.94%，且泛化能力强；真实恶意软件样本实验验证了其实际有效性。

Conclusion: Hieronym 借助 LLM 与多源信息融合，有效解决剥离二进制函数重命名难题，兼具高性能与强泛化性，在安全关键场景具备实用价值。

Abstract: Function renaming in stripped binaries can substantially assist reverse engineers by improving code readability, yet it is a challenging task. The difficulty stems from the need to accurately capture function semantics from low-level binary code across diverse instruction sets, architectures, and compiler optimizations, and to express these semantics in concise, human-readable names. Existing approaches either inadequately capture comprehensive function semantics or exhibit limited generalization to previously unseen binaries. In this paper, we present Hieronym, a generative large language model (LLM)-based framework for stripped binary function renaming. Hieronym adopts a hierarchical summarization-driven domain adaptation strategy and integrates multi-source information, including global binary context, local calling context, and intrinsic function semantics, to enhance the LLM's understanding of binary code. To enable systematic evaluation, we further propose a dual-layer evaluation framework that incorporates both token-level and whole-name-level metrics. We evaluate Hieronym on binary functions compiled with four compiler optimization levels (O0-O3) for four architectures (x64, x86, ARM, and MIPS). Experimental results demonstrate that Hieronym significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving token-level improvements of 50.12% in precision, 41.75% in recall, and 45.10% in F1-score, as well as a 79.94% improvement in name-level accuracy, while also exhibiting strong generalization capability. Moreover, experiments on real-world malware samples further validate the practical effectiveness of Hieronym in security-critical scenarios.

</details>


### [124] [What is the Difference Between Me and You? Benchmarking the Quality Gap Between Human-Written and AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12708)
*Cristina Improta,Pietro Liguori,Domenico Cotroneo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 大规模对比人写与AI生成代码（787,562对函数，Python/Java/C），发现AI代码结构更紧凑、风格模板化，缺陷与安全特征因语言而异，并发布CQBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手已成为生产软件的共同作者，但现有评估只关注功能正确性，未回答AI代码在主导生命周期成本的质量维度（复杂度、风格、缺陷、安全）上是否与人类代码不同。

Method: 从开源仓库挖掘人类函数，并用三个AI助手（OpenAI GPT、DeepSeek-Coder、Qwen2.5-Coder）依据其docstring生成实现，构成Python/Java/C共787,562对函数。刻画结构复杂度与统计自然性，并将静态分析结果分别映射到正交缺陷分类（ODC，缺陷）和CWE（漏洞），实现作者与语言的横向可比。

Result: AI代码结构压缩、风格模板化：规模与分支约为人类的一半，风格层面聚类明显分离。缺陷类型不同：人类集中于成熟代码库问题，AI集中于重复样板。安全性依赖语言：Python和Java中LLM产生更多且更严重的问题，C中高严重度内存安全发现少于人类。控制规模后复杂度指标信号微弱，而自然性可有效区分作者。

Conclusion: AI与人类代码在质量维度上存在系统性差异，且差异随语言与维度而异；仅看功能正确性不足以评估AI代码质量。作者发布CQBench（27,346个易出问题任务、基线与评估管线），支持质量保障与安全测试。

Abstract: AI coding assistants are becoming co-authors of production software, yet their evaluation centers on functional correctness, leaving open whether their code differs from human code in the quality dimensions dominating lifecycle cost. We compare human-written and AI-generated code at scale: 787,562 function pairs across Python, Java, and C, each human function mined from open-source repositories paired with implementations generated from its docstring by three AI assistants (OpenAI GPT models, DeepSeek-Coder, Qwen2.5-Coder). We characterize structural complexity and statistical naturalness, and map static-analysis findings onto Orthogonal Defect Classification for defects and the Common Weakness Enumeration for vulnerabilities, making authors and languages directly comparable. AI-generated code is structurally compressed and stylistically templated: roughly half the size and branching of human code, clustering apart at the style level. Defect profiles differ in kind: human code concentrates issues of mature codebases, AI code repetitive boilerplate; security is language-dependent, with LLMs producing more, and more severe, findings in Python and Java but fewer high-severity memory-safety findings than humans in C. Once size is controlled for, complexity metrics carry little signal, while naturalness separates authors. Finally, we release CQBench, a benchmark of 27,346 issue-prone tasks with baselines and an evaluation pipeline for quality assurance and security testing.

</details>


### [125] [GraphAHA: Graph-Based Adaptive Search with Heterogeneous Actions for Test-Time Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12757)
*Xitao Li,Haijun Wang,Gege Yuan,Qiyuan Wu,Jiali Wei,Ming Fan,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出GraphAHA，用带类型的有向无环图组织测试时代码生成，合并等价程序节点并复用统计量，用分层Thompson采样自适应分配采样/推理/实现/修复动作，在多个基准上取得最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展可通过采样、反馈修复和推理引导提升代码生成，但存在两个问题：一是树状搜索将不同历史视为不同状态，即使轨迹收敛到同一程序也重复评估、无法共享统计信息；二是采样、修复、推理的收益互补且依赖状态，在有限预算下难以在线分配。

Method: GraphAHA将测试时代码生成组织为带类型的DAG，将等价程序合并为单一代码节点，使下游搜索统计量可在所有发现路径间复用；再用分层Thompson采样决定生成新状态还是沿已有后继节点，并在生成时选择类型有效的采样、推理、实现和修复操作。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上，使用Qwen2.5-Coder和DeepSeek-Coder评估，20个案例中18个取得最佳分数；可见测试下的Pass@1相对最强基线平均提升4.1个百分点。

Conclusion: GraphAHA通过等价程序合并与分层Thompson采样，实现了对固定推理预算更有效的利用，优于现有基线。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves code generation by spending additional inference budget (e.g., calls or tokens) on direct sampling, feedback-conditioned repair, and reasoning-guided implementation. Search-based methods can allocate this budget adaptively, but two challenges remain. First, tree-structured search treats each generation history as a separate state even when trajectories converge to the same program, duplicating evaluation and preventing statistics from being shared. Second, sampling, repair, and reasoning have complementary and state-dependent payoffs, making online allocation among them difficult under a finite budget. To address these challenges, we propose an adaptive graph search method with heterogeneous actions (GraphAHA). GraphAHA organizes the test-time code generation in a typed directed acyclic graph. Equivalent programs are merged into a single code node, allowing their downstream search statistics to be reused across all discovery paths. Hierarchical Thompson sampling then selects whether to generate a new state or follow an existing successor and, for generation, chooses among the type-valid sampling, reasoning, implementation, and repair operations. Evaluated on LiveCodeBench and CodeContests with Qwen2.5-Coder and DeepSeek-Coder, GraphAHA achieves the best score in 18 of 20 cases. For Pass@1 measured using visible tests, it outperforms the strongest baseline for both models on both benchmarks by 4.1 percentage points on average, demonstrating more effective use of a fixed inference budget.

</details>


### [126] [Detecting HTTP Status Code Misuses in REST APIs via Static and Dynamic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12770)
*Alix Decrop,Andrea Arcuri,Mike Papadakis,Pierre-Yves Schobbens,Gilles Perrouin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出结合静态与动态分析的方法，检测 REST API 中 HTTP 状态码的误用，基于 2,625 个真实 API 规范归纳出 30 条使用规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REST 基于 HTTP，状态码语义未被强制，导致实践中普遍误用（如用 500 表示客户端错误），会降低互操作性、误导客户端、造成测试误报。

Method: 先研究 2,625 个真实 REST API 规范，识别相关状态码并基于 HTTP 标准与 REST 原则推导 30 条使用规则；再实现工具，对 OpenAPI 规范做静态分析、对 API 实际行为做动态分析。

Result: 评估发现状态码误用在 REST API 中频繁且系统性存在，静态与动态方法均能检测出多种误用。

Conclusion: 静态与动态方法可互补使用，研究为 REST API 测试者与使用者提供了见解。

Abstract: REST APIs are widely used on the web for client-server communications. As REST is based on HTTP, server responses contain status codes to indicate the outcome of requests (e.g., 200 OK for a success and 404 Not Found for an unavailable resource). While HTTP status codes are standardized, their semantics are not enforced in REST, leading to many misuses in practice (e.g., using 500 Internal Server Error to describe a client error). Such misuses may have nefarious consequences, such as reducing interoperability, misleading API clients, or causing false positives in testing tools. In this paper, we present a combined static and dynamic analysis approach for detecting HTTP status code misuses in REST APIs. We first study 2,625 real-world REST API specifications to identify relevant status codes and derive a set of 30 usage rules based on HTTP standards and REST API principles. We then implement tools to identify such rule violations in OpenAPI specifications (static analysis) and in API behavior (dynamic analysis). Our evaluation finds that status code misuses are frequent and systematic in REST APIs, with both static and dynamic approaches detecting various misuses. We highlight that both approaches may be used in a complementary manner, and also provide insight for REST API testers and users alike.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Establishing the Four-Day Workweek: Understanding Adaptation and Long-Term Survival in an Agile Software Organization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13089)
*Michael Neumann,Darja Šmite*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过纵向单案例研究，考察一家敏捷软件组织如何在32小时四天工作制下引入、适应、制度化并长期维持该安排，提出4DWW生命周期模型与"生存矩阵"两个概念工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有四天工作制研究多聚焦于引入阶段与短期效应，对其长期存续机制及其与敏捷软件开发的交互作用理解不足。

Method: 对一家实行32小时四天工作周的软件组织进行纵向单案例研究，基于2022年与2026年共15次半结构化访谈，采用定性内容分析。

Result: 4DWW应被视为持续演化的安排而非一次性干预：引入后团队重构协调、沟通、会议、敏捷实践与迭代；制度化后面对所有权变更、经济下行及市场与AI压力，员工通过自愿保护性调整吸收压力，而非回到五天制，并预期回退将损害工作满意度、组织承诺与雇主形象。

Conclusion: 研究提供敏捷软件开发中维持4DWW的纵向证据，并提出4DWW生命周期模型与4DWW生存矩阵，解释外部压力与管理层思路如何影响回退风险。

Abstract: Context: Existing research on the four-day workweek (4DWW) has primarily examined its introduction and short-term effects, with limited understanding of its long-term survival or its interaction with agile software development. Objective: We study how a reduced-hour 4DWW is introduced, adapted, institutionalized, and sustained under changing organizational and external conditions in an agile software organization. Method: We conducted a longitudinal single-case study of a software organization operating a 32-hour, four-day week. The study draws on 15 semi-structured interviews in 2022 and 2026, analyzed using qualitative content analysis. Results: The 4DWW is better understood as an evolving arrangement than a one-off intervention. After the introduction, teams redesigned coordination, communication, meetings, agile practices, and iterations to adapt to reduced working time. Once institutionalized, the 4DWW faced ownership change, economic downturn, and market and AI pressures. Rather than reverting to five-day workweek, employees absorbed these pressures through voluntary protective adaptations, while anticipating that a rollback would harm job satisfaction, organizational commitment, and employer image. Contribution: We contribute longitudinal evidence on sustaining 4DWW in agile software development and propose two conceptual artifacts: a lifecycle model of the 4DWW and a 4DWW survival matrix, explaining how external pressures and management rationale rollback risk.

</details>


### [128] [Is Bash All You Need? An Empirical Study of Tool Interfaces for Enterprise Digital Worker Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11999)
*Hazel Mak,Susheel Suresh,Sahil Bhatnagar,Barry Wang,Chhaya Methani,Alejandro Gutierrez Munoz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究比较五种工具接口在企业任务上的表现，发现单独使用bash优于类型化工具，且更省token；PTC适合需要固定工具目录的安全场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shell-based agents在编码任务上表现优异，但企业工作还需在应用与服务间切换、与同事协作、进行专业分析，因此需要检验通用shell能否在企业任务上超越专用工具。

Method: 在TheAgentCompany和APEX-Agents两个基准上，用Opus-4.8和GPT-5.5比较五种工具接口：类型化工具、类型化工具+bash、单独bash、带持久化agent合成工具的bash、程序化工具调用(PTC，程序动作限于类型化工具目录)。

Result: 单独bash在两个基准上均优于类型化工具，TheAgentCompany提升21.8-24.5pp、APEX-Agents提升4.8-7.4pp，且总token减少19-72%。加入类型化工具或持久化工具合成无显著得分增益。PTC比直接类型化调用省token、任务表现大致相当，但在质量和成本效率上通常不如单独bash。

Conclusion: 对企业实践者而言，当可隔离任意执行时推荐单独bash；当安全或合规政策要求固定工具目录时推荐PTC。

Abstract: In this study, we examine whether a general shell can outperform specialized tools on enterprise tasks. Shell-based agents have shown strong results in coding, but enterprise work also involves moving between applications and services, coordinating with coworkers, and performing professional analysis. We compare five tool interfaces on TheAgentCompany and APEX-Agents using Opus-4.8 and GPT-5.5: typed tools, typed tools plus bash, bash alone, bash with persistent agent-synthesized tools, and programmatic tool calling (PTC), which runs programs whose actions are restricted to a typed tool catalog. Bash alone outperforms typed tools on both benchmarks, improving score by 21.8-24.5 pp on TheAgentCompany and 4.8-7.4 pp on APEX-Agents while using 19-72% fewer total tokens. Adding typed tools or persistent tool synthesis to bash produces no detectable pooled score gain. PTC uses fewer tokens than direct typed calls with broadly similar task performance, but generally underperforms bash alone in both quality and cost efficiency. For enterprise practitioners, these results favor bash alone when arbitrary execution can be isolated and PTC when security or compliance policies require a fixed tool catalog.

</details>


### [129] [Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Scientific Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12190)
*Aaron Nobile,Andreas Adelmann,Mohsen Sadr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建一个完全本地的科学代码理解RAG系统，通过将重计算转移到离线阶段，使9B小模型在C++科学代码基准上超越更大模型及Claude Code架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前先进编码助手（如Claude Code、Codex）依赖大型云端模型，计算成本高且存在数据隐私问题，因此探索能否用小型开源模型构建实用的全本地编码代理。

Method: 提出面向科学代码理解的RAG系统，严格分离昂贵的离线摄取阶段（解析、结构图构建、LLM实体解释生成、嵌入）与轻量在线回答阶段。在IPPL C++科学代码库的100题基准（11类）上评估，由独立前沿模型作为评判。

Result: 在七种回答模型中，模型家族与检索质量比参数规模更重要：9B模型取得最高平均分0.795，超越管道内更大模型及嵌入Claude Code检索架构的相同模型。

Conclusion: 将代码理解前置到可复用、代码库专用的向量存储中，能让小型本地模型给出有依据、针对特定仓库的回答，适合作为内部科学代码库的隐私保护开发工具。

Abstract: Large language models have become central to modern coding assistants, but state-of-the-art systems such as Claude Code or Codex rely on very large, cloud-hosted models with significant computational cost and data-privacy implications. This work investigates whether a useful, fully local coding agent can be built around small open-source models by shifting the computational burden away from inference. We develop a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for scientific code understanding that strictly separates an expensive offline ingestion stage parsing, structural graph construction, LLM-generated entity explanations, and embedding from a lightweight online answering stage. The system is evaluated on a 100-question benchmark spanning eleven categories over the IPPL scientific codebase written in C++, with answers scored by an independent frontier model as the judge. Across seven answering models, we find that model family and retrieval quality matter more than parameter count, i.e. a 9B model achieves the highest average score (0.795), outperforming both larger models within our pipeline and the same models embedded in the Claude Code retrieval architecture. The results indicate that front-loading code understanding into a reusable, codebase-specialised vector store enables small local models to deliver grounded and repository-specific answers, making the agent well suited as a privacy-preserving development tool for in-house scientific codebases.

</details>


### [130] [Separating Engineering Reasoning from DEXPI Serialization in LLM-Based Greenfield Surface-Process Design: A Three-Case Study for Underground Gas Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12656)
*Qingchuan Zhu,Shuyue Tong,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项诊断性研究比较"直接生成DEXPI"与"先生成轻量工程中间表示(IR)"两种方式，发现分离串行化能大幅降低表示负担并有助于隔离串行化失败，但无法消除工程不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可生成工程描述与结构化工艺表示，但DEXPI标准级串行化会显著增加生成负担；研究欲探究"将工程推理与DEXPI串行化分离"是否会改变表示失败与工程失败的分布。

Method: 针对地下储气库绿地表面工艺设计的三个案例（单压注入、采出与外输、双压注入），对比直接DEXPI生成与轻量工程中间表示（IR）生成。六个条件均使用同一固定模型快照qwen3.8-max-0902，每条件完成一次托管生成。

Result: 直接输出3例中2例通过XSD校验；3例Engineering IR输出在最小验证器下均结构有效。直接提示输入约121.8k–121.9k tokens，IR提示仅617–699 tokens。工程可行性并非一律偏向IR：有1例IR输出因明确的冷却状态矛盾被拒。直接DEXPI出现两种失败模式：工程不一致与标准级串行化失败。

Conclusion: 在本文评估的方法组合中，推迟DEXPI串行化可显著降低表示负担并帮助隔离串行化失败，但仅降低表示负担并不能消除工程不一致。

Abstract: Large language models can produce engineering descriptions and structured process representations, but standards-level serialization can substantially increase the generation burden. This diagnostic study examines whether separating engineering reasoning from Data Exchange in the Process Industry (DEXPI) serialization changes where representation and engineering failures occur in constrained greenfield surface-process design for underground gas storage. We compare Direct DEXPI generation with generation of a lightweight Engineering Intermediate Representation (IR) on three cases: single-pressure injection, withdrawal and export, and dual-pressure injection. All six conditions use one fixed model snapshot, qwen3.8-max-0902, with one completed hosted generation per condition. Direct outputs are XSD-valid in 2 of 3 cases, while all 3 Engineering IR outputs are structurally valid under a minimal validator. Direct prompt inputs contain approximately 121.8k-121.9k tokens, compared with 617-699 tokens for the Engineering IR prompts. Engineering feasibility does not uniformly favor the IR: one IR output is rejected for an explicit cooling-state contradiction. The cases also reveal two distinct Direct DEXPI failure modes: engineering inconsistency and standards-level serialization failure. The observed comparison shows that, in these evaluated method bundles, deferring DEXPI serialization substantially reduces representation burden and helps isolate serialization failure, but reducing representation burden alone does not eliminate engineering inconsistencies.

</details>


### [131] [Involving before Evolving: A Vision for Trustworthy Enterprise Digital Twin Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13071)
*Kérian Fiter,Adil Lagrou,Franck Dervault,Bentley Oakes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可信企业数字孪生（EDT）工程愿景，核心是"先参与、后演进"范式：通过可运行原型快速促成利益相关者参与，再逐步走向联邦与互操作。方法分三阶段：基础模型快速原型、本体检地支撑联邦互操作、可观测性工具建立信任。初步原型已在米其林合作中支持了利益相关者认同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDT可实现组织级数据驱动决策，但落地需应对部门孤岛、隐性知识及高风险长周期决策。现有方法在模型开发中引入领域专家，却较少关注EDT早期的组织认同问题。

Method: 提出三阶段方法：1）用基础模型进行快速原型开发；2）以本体检地（ontological backbone）支撑联邦互操作；3）通过可观测性工具建立利益相关者信任。

Result: 在与跨国制造商米其林的持续合作中，初始原型已帮助支持了利益相关者的认同。

Conclusion: EDT工程应以"involving before evolving"为核心，优先通过原型促成参与，再演进至联邦和全面互操作，从而实现可信的EDT。

Abstract: Enterprise Digital Twins (EDTs) promise data-driven decision support at organizational scale, but realizing them requires navigating siloed departments, tacit knowledge, and high-stakes decisions with long-horizon consequences. Existing approaches involve domain experts during model development but focus less on early organizational buy-in in EDTs. We present a vision for trustworthy EDT engineering grounded in an `involving before evolving' paradigm: rapidly involving stakeholders through a working prototype before evolving toward federation and full interoperability. Our three-stage approach combines foundation models for rapid prototyping, an ontological backbone for federated interoperability, and observability tooling for stakeholder trust. We ground our vision in an ongoing collaboration with Michelin, a multinational manufacturer, where an initial prototype has helped support stakeholder buy-in.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [NDT Factory: Synthesizing Verified Network Digital Twins from Semantic Models via Multi-Agent LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12170)
*Sudipta Acharya,Petar Djukic,Burak Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出 "NDT factory"，一个多智能体系统，利用 LLM 按需从语义模型合成可执行的行为网络数字孪生（NDT），以实现自主网络管理中的意图评估，并通过 CAC 案例验证其可靠性与确定性执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TM Forum L4 自治要求系统能在无需人工实现分析逻辑的情况下评估网络服务意图（NSI）；现有行为 NDT 依赖预定义分析逻辑，难以适应持续演进的闭环控制。

Method: 构建 NDT factory 多智能体软件系统，基于 LLM 从语义模型按需合成可执行的行为 NDT，并通过并行合成与编排生成完整 NDT。以呼叫准入控制（CAC）为案例，用确定性的 what-if 分析作为准入决策过程进行验证。

Result: 多次运行中编译与测试通过率均达 100%；对 300 个 NSI 的仿真显示与参考实现决策一致率 99.3%，准入率 90%，所有拒绝均被正确归因。

Conclusion: NDT factory 能可靠地按需合成行为 NDT，实现确定性且可验证的执行，为面向 L4 自治的网络意图评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous network management requires systems that can evaluate Network Service Intents (NSIs) under varying conditions without manual implementation of analysis logic, as envisioned in TM Forum Level~4 (L4) autonomy. Behavioral Network Digital Twins (NDTs) enable such evaluation, but existing NDTs rely on pre-defined analytical logic, limiting adaptability for evolving closed-loop control. This paper introduces the NDT factory, a multi-agent software system that synthesizes executable behavioral NDTs on demand from semantic models using Large Language Model (LLM). We validate the system using a Call Admission Control (CAC) case study, where deterministic what-if analysis serves as the admission decision process. The NDT factory generates a complete CAC NDT through parallel synthesis and orchestration, achieving 100% compilation and test pass rates across multiple runs. Simulation over 300 NSIs shows 99.3% decision agreement with a reference implementation, 90% admission rate, and correct attribution of all rejections, demonstrating reliable synthesis with deterministic, verifiable execution.

</details>


### [133] [Virtualized 5G Tesbed using OpenAirInterface: Tutorial and Benchmarking Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12972)
*M. Dória,V. Sousa,A. Campos,N. Oliveira,P. Eduardo,P. Filho,C. Lima,J. Guilherme,D. Luna,I. Rego,M. Fernandes,A. Neto*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提供基于 OpenAirInterface (OAI) 5G 栈与 USRP 平台的 5G 网络教程，涵盖核心网和无线接入网的部署与实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G 及向 6G 演进将虚拟化推向天线端，但 RF 前端仍为专有硬件。需要利用软件灵活性同时保持微秒级信号处理性能，因此探讨 OAI 在 USRP 上的 5G 原型实现。

Method: 提供 OAI 5G 栈在 USRP 平台上核心网与 RAN 的教程材料，包含安装部署说明、配置讨论和吞吐量基准分析，展示不同传输能力及与商用手机兼容的蓝图。

Result: 实验显示使用 OAI 和 USRP 前端的 5G 原型具有良好可复现性和一致的吞吐量。

Conclusion: OAI 与 USRP 组合可用于可复现、稳定吞吐的 5G 原型验证。

Abstract: The development of 5G and its evolutionary path to 6G brings virtualization as close as possible to the antennas. Native 3GPP systems are now software running at servers boosted by accelerator cards to cope with computationally intense signal processing. Meanwhile, the Radio Frequency (RF) front-end is still proprietary hardware, sheltering specific PHY-layer procedures like passband amplification/modulation. This approach takes advantage of the software's flexibility while keeping the complex microsecond signal processing performance from modern telecommunication systems. This paper provides tutorial material on the Core Network and Radio Access Network of OpenAirInterface (OAI) 5G stack on top of Universal Software Radio Peripheral (USRP) platforms. A set of blueprints showcases OAI's ability to provide a virtualized 5G network with different transmission capabilities and the possibility to use it with commercial mobile phones. Configuration discussions and throughput benchmark analyses follow installation and deployment instructions. Our results show that 5G prototyping using OAI and USRP frontends can lead to good reproducibility and consistent throughput.

</details>


### [134] [On Identifying Adversarial Intent Injection in AI-Native 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12144)
*Nilesh Chakraborty,Petar Djukic,Burak Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文将Intent-Based Networking的对抗性意图注入威胁建模为AI-native 6G网络中的攻击面，提出双路径检测框架（CNN+TF-IDF监督检测与AutoEncoder单类检测），并研究四种恶意意图注入策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IBN使高层目标可转化为网络配置，但带来新的攻击面，主要是对抗性意图注入——恶意策略伪装在良性意图流中。若采用隐蔽模式注入，检测难度显著增加。因此需要细粒度威胁模型与有效检测方法。

Method: 首先定义细粒度的威胁模型以刻画AI-native网络中的恶意意图注入。研究四种注入策略：隐蔽模式、随机分布、频率递增、频率递减。提出双路径检测框架：(i) 基于TF-IDF特征的CNN进行监督式恶意意图检测；(ii) 仅在良性数据上训练的AutoEncoder进行单类恶意意图检测。

Result: 评估显示检测性能强：准确率提升至0.97（相较SOTA基线约+9%），F1-score提升至0.98（约+36%）。

Conclusion: 该双路径框架能有效检测多种恶意意图注入策略，在准确率与F1上显著优于现有最优基线，验证了在AI-native 6G网络中防御对抗性意图注入的可行性。

Abstract: AI-native 6G networks have brought Intent-Based Networking (IBN) to the forefront, enabling high-level goals to be translated into network configurations. However, this abstraction opens new attack surfaces, primarily adversarial intent injection, where malicious policies are disguised within benign intent flows. The detection of attack instances might become significantly more difficult if the adversaries adopt a stealthy mode of malicious intent injection. With all these in mind, we first define a fine-grained threat model that facilitates the threat of malicious intent injection in an AI-native network. Alongside, we investigate four malicious intent injection strategies$-$ stealth-mode, random distribution, increasing frequency, and decreasing frequency- and propose a dual-path detection framework: (i) a CNN using TF-IDF features for supervised malicious intent detection, and (ii) an AutoEncoder trained exclusively on benign data for one-class malicious intent detection. Our evaluation demonstrates strong detection performance, with accuracy improving to 0.97 (~9\% gain) and F1-score to 0.98 (~36\% gain) over the state-of-the-art baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Occamy-1.0: Open Pareto-frontier 35B Intelligence for Co-work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11977)
*Wenhui Chen,Shiwen Cheng,Hao Dong,Chenda Duan,Ruixiang Feng,Zhong Guan,Boqiang Guo,Xueyuan Han,Haojie Hao,Liangmeng Huang,Zhelong Huang,Xinke Kong,Hongyu Li,Jiazheng Li,Junbo Li,Qingchuan Li,Yukun Lian,Chang Liu,Tianyu Liu,Zicheng Liu,Shuyi Ouyang,Yijun Pan,Kunyu Shi,Xiaojun Tang,Bingquan Wang,Kesu Wang,Yuchen Wang,Sibo Wei,Sicong Xie,Xiaoying Xing,Yi Xu,Zhijun Xu,Hongwei Xue,Qingcheng Zeng,Di Zhang,Guannan Zhang,Haochen Zhang,Tianlong Zhang,Tianyu Zhao,Tianyu Zhao,Yanjun Zheng,Jialong Zhu,Zijian Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Occamy-1.0，一个基于 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 继续训练的高性价比协作智能体模型，在多个基准上达到同等规模模型领先水平，并处于成本-性能帕累托前沿的低成本拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作智能体需跨多次模型调用完成信息收集、工具使用、编码与文件操作等复杂工作流，成本与延迟随整个回合累积，因此实用价值不仅取决于峰值能力，还取决于交付效率。日常工作中很多步骤侧重状态跟踪、协调、恢复与任务跟进，而非前沿级推理，故需成本高效的专用模型。

Method: 以训练后的 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 检查点为起点继续训练；构建基于执行的数据与环境，跨多种 harness 采集可回放的长时程轨迹，并采用分阶段后训练以培养和巩固互补的执行能力。

Result: 在广泛协作基准上持续处于同等规模模型最强之列，部分任务可与更大前沿系统竞争；在给定评估与定价协议下，四项代表性基准的综合表现位于成本-性能帕累托前沿的低成本拐点；工具调用、编码与指令遵循等辅助评测显示该专精未损害广泛智能体能力。

Conclusion: Occamy-1.0 验证了面向实际协作场景的高性价比智能体后训练路径，并开源模型权重与部分训练数据，以推动实用协作智能体与智能体后训练研究。

Abstract: Co-work agents execute complex workflows that combine information gathering, tool use, coding, and file manipulation across many model invocations. Because cost and latency accumulate over the full episode, their practical value depends not only on peak capability but also on how efficiently that capability is delivered. Yet many steps in everyday work emphasize state tracking, coordination, recovery, and follow-through rather than frontier-scale reasoning. We present Occamy-1.0, a cost-efficient co-work model obtained by further training the post-trained Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoint. We construct execution-grounded data and environments, capture replayable long-horizon trajectories across multiple harnesses, and use staged post-training to develop and consolidate complementary execution capabilities. Across a broad suite of co-work benchmarks, Occamy-1.0 is consistently among the strongest comparably sized models and remains competitive with substantially larger frontier systems on several tasks. Under our stated evaluation and pricing protocol, its aggregate performance across four representative benchmarks places it at the low-cost knee of the observed cost--performance Pareto frontier. Supporting evaluations in tool calling, coding, and instruction following further show that this specialization preserves broad agentic capability. We release the model weights and a subset of the training data to support research on practical co-work agents and agentic post-training.

</details>


### [136] [Reading the Whole Heart: Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders for Multimodal Cardiac Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12035)
*Andrea Agostini,Simon Böhi,Moritz Vandenhirtz,Samuel Ruiperez-Campillo,Max Krähenmann,Silke Mühlstedt,Irene Cannistraci,Ece Özkan Elsen,Julia E. Vogt,Thomas M. Sutter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LAMAE，一种多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练中直接在潜空间交换信息，学习患者级表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管诊断依赖ECG、超声、胸片和临床变量等互补模态，但多数医学基础模型仍是单模态的，仅在微调或后训练阶段融合，丢弃了临床医生自然整合的跨模态证据及各模态内部结构。

Method: LAMAE通过共享的潜注意力模块在study-view-entity层级结构上运作，直接在潜空间交换信息，实现变量观测聚合并优雅处理缺失模态，而非事后融合。基于超120万MIMIC-IV住院数据进行预训练。

Result: 在多模态住院任务（院内死亡、ICD-10与DRG编码、住院时长）上优于单模态预训练及强对比学习和视觉-语言基线，单模态任务保持竞争力；即使测试时仅单一模态可用，增益依然存在。

Conclusion: 同时建模模态内和模态间结构可产生更鲁棒、可迁移的表示。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diagnosis rests on integrating complementary modalities, like ECG, echocardiography, chest radiographs, and clinical variables, each capturing distinct but correlated aspects of cardiac physiology. Yet most medical foundation models remain modality-specific, combining modalities only for finetuning or post-training. This discards the cross-modal evidence clinicians naturally integrate and ignores the structure within each modality. We introduce Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE), a multimodal, structure-aware masked autoencoder that jointly learns patient-level representations during self-supervised pretraining. Rather than fusing modalities post hoc, LAMAE exchanges information directly in the latent space through a shared latent-attention module operating over a study-view-entity hierarchy, enabling aggregation of variable observations and graceful handling of missing modalities. Pretrained on over 1.2 million MIMIC-IV hospital stays, LAMAE outperforms modality-specific pretraining and strong contrastive and vision-language baselines across multimodal hospital-stay tasks, such as in-hospital mortality, ICD-10 and DRG coding, and length of stay, while remaining competitive on unimodal tasks. These gains persist even when only a single modality is available at test time, showing that modeling both intra- and inter-modal structure yields more robust, transferable representations.

</details>


### [137] [Language Is an Insufficient Substrate for Quantitative Reasoning, and Consequential Domains Need Large Quantitative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12105)
*Reuben Vandeventer,David Imrem,David J. Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"大型定量模型"(LQM)新类别，论证语言模型因训练于有损的文本描述，无法胜任高风险定量决策，需从表征层面重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流ML界假定LLM的进步会自然带来定价风险、资本配置、分诊病人、网络入侵遏制等重大定量决策的进步。作者质疑此假设：LLM训练于人类描述构成的世界表征，而描述是定量记录的"有损编码"，且损失不可逆——任何下游模型无论规模多大都无法从描述中恢复未被编码的信息。

Method: 将问题形式化为"模型所训练的表征"属性，而非模型容量属性。并指出高风险场景对模型有三项语言基底无法先天提供的要求：可复现性、从每个输出回溯至源记录的谱系、校准的不确定性。

Result: 论证了语言基底在构造上无法满足上述三项属性，因此这些属性定义了一个独立的模型类别。

Conclusion: 符合这些属性要求的模型构成新的模型类别，作者称之为"大型定量模型"(Large Quantitative Model, LQM)。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in applied machine learning is that progress on consequential quantitative decisions such as pricing risk, allocating capital, triaging patients, or containing a network intrusion will follow from progress in large language models (LLMs). A language model is trained on a representation of the world that was produced by human description; description is a lossy encoding of the quantitative record, and the loss is irreversible: no downstream model, at any scale, can recover from a description what the description did not encode. We formalize this as a property of the representation on which a model is trained rather than of the model capacity, and we identify three further properties that consequential settings demand of a model and that a language substrate cannot supply by construction: reproducibility, lineage from every output back to the source records that produced. it, and calibrated uncertainty. We argue that these properties define a distinct model class, which we call the Large Quantitative Model (LQM).

</details>


### [138] [DU-NO: A Parameter-Efficient Double U-Shaped Neural Operator for Phase-Resolving Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12115)
*Enrique Hernandez Noguera,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Nathan Cooper,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DU-NO，一种双U形多尺度谱神经算子，仅在编码/解码最浅两层加轻量卷积分支，3.64M参数下在近岸波浪、2D Navier-Stokes和浅水方程上达到或超越大型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位解析波模型（如FUNWAVE-TVD）精度高但计算昂贵，无法支撑集合预报、不确定性量化和实时预警；而现有高保真神经算子（如U-FNO）参数量达千万级，代价过大。

Method: 提出DU-NO：多尺度U形谱算子，仅在最浅的编码器和解码器两层附加轻量卷积U-Net分支。依据采样理论——高波数内容只存在于细网格上，故局部全频带通路布置在高频所在层，粗尺度层保持纯谱处理；并采用深度衰减的模态调度控制参数量。

Result: 参数量3.64M（比U-FNO少一个数量级），在FUNWAVE-TVD基准上自回归误差优于6个同训练架构，比U-FNO改善14.9%且参数少10.8倍；频带分析显示各频带均改善（含高频带）；参数匹配对照中最佳基线仍落后28.6%；在2D Navier-Stokes上持平最强基线，在PDEBench浅水方程上明显胜出。

Conclusion: DU-NO以极小参数量实现谱算子级精度，优势源于架构设计而非参数规模，并可推广至近岸波浪之外的多种PDE场景。

Abstract: Phase-resolving wave models such as FUNWAVE-TVD are the accuracy standard for nearshore dynamics, resolving the shoaling, refraction, and breaking of individual waves, but their cost rules them out for the ensembles, uncertainty quantification, and real-time warning that operational forecasting demands. Neural operators promise solver-level accuracy at a fraction of that cost, yet on wave-dominated fields the accurate ones are large: hybrid spectral-convolutional operators such as U-FNO (the strongest baseline in our study after DU-NO) buy their fidelity with tens of millions of parameters. We introduce DU-NO (Double U-shaped Neural Operator), a multiscale U-shaped spectral operator that attaches lightweight convolutional U-Net branches only at its two shallowest encoder and decoder levels. The placement follows a sampling argument: high-wavenumber content exists only on fine grids, so the local, full-band pathways go where that content lives, while the coarse, band-limited levels stay purely spectral. A depth-decaying mode schedule holds the model to 3.64M parameters, an order of magnitude below U-FNO. On our publicly released FUNWAVE-TVD benchmark, DU-NO attains the best autoregressive rollout error of six identically trained architectures, improving on U-FNO by 14.9% with 10.8x fewer parameters, and a frequency-band analysis shows the gain holds across all bands, including the high-wavenumber band where truncated-spectral operators collapse. Parameter-matched controls confirm the gain is architectural: rescaled to the same 3.6M budget, the best baseline still trails DU-NO by 28.6%. The advantage carries beyond nearshore waves: DU-NO matches the strongest baselines on 2D Navier-Stokes and wins clearly on PDEBench shallow-water rollouts. Code, trained models, and evaluation artifacts are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/duno-code-5A7B/.

</details>


### [139] [Mined from Scientific Literature: Process Schemas for Atomic Layer Deposition and Etching in Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12139)
*Sameer Sadruddin,Eleni Poupaki,Alex Watkins,Bora Karasulu,Adriaan J. M. Mackus,Erwin Kessels,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出四个经领域专家评审的JSON Schema，用于标准化ALD和ALE的实验与模拟过程数据，基于QUDT语义框架，支持文献抽取与结构化发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALD和ALE的实验与模拟文献在材料科学领域中异构严重，缺乏统一表示，阻碍了数据比较与机器可操作复用。

Method: 使用schema-miner和schema-miner pro工具，基于QUDT本体构建四个JSON Schema，涵盖材料、工艺条件、配置及测量/预测结果；并比较其范围、结构与语义基础。

Result: 四个Schema经领域专家评审；展示了其在schema引导的文献抽取及通过ORKG模板发布结构化记录中的应用。

Conclusion: 该工作为ALD/ALE领域提供语义化、可复用的数据模式，促进文献数据的标准化比较与机器可操作复用。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) and atomic layer etching (ALE) are reported heterogeneously across experimental and simulation literature in materials science, hindering comparison and machine-actionable reuse. We present four domain-expert-reviewed JSON Schemas for ALD and ALE experimental and simulation processes. Curated with schema-miner and grounded in QUDT using schema-miner pro, the schemas structure materials, process conditions, configurations , and measured or predicted results. We compare their scope, structure, and semantic grounding, and demonstrate their use for schema-guided literature extraction and publication of structured records through ORKG templates.

</details>


### [140] [Can LLMs in Draft-Verify-Revise Pipelines Resolve Deictic Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12162)
*Obinna I. Ekekezie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"指示性漂移"(deictic shift)现象：在draft-verify-revise流水线中，跨阶段的上下文级联会导致"previous"等依赖上下文的表达在不同LLM阶段指向不同对象。作者用合成数据集测试六个模型、21种推理配置，发现平衡准确率从低于随机的0.156到接近完美，且模型推理努力程度强烈影响表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Draft-verify-revise是扩展推理时计算的常见LLM编排模式。但上下文在阶段间级联传递时，不同阶段的LLM可能对"previous"等上下文依赖表达产生不同解读，即发生指示性漂移（所指对象的改变）。这种漂移对流水线准确性的影响此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建含10个基础样本、每样本3种条件的合成数据集：在共享组件不变的条件下，变化draft阶段（assistant）或verify阶段（grader）是否正确解析表达，以及revise阶段（meta-evaluator）需要多少独立推理来判断正确读法。测试来自3个供应商的6个模型、21种推理努力配置，使用e-values进行序贯检验。设主实验和消融实验（从grader反馈中移除错误分类标签）。另用一个LLM分析meta-evaluator给出错误判定时陈述的理由。

Result: 平衡准确率（灵敏度与特异度的非加权均值）跨度极大，从低于随机的0.156到接近完美。GPT-5.2从无推理时的0.156升至最高推理努力时的0.942；Gemini 3 Pro在所有努力级别均保持在0.94以上。Gemini 3 Pro在低推理努力下的表现超过GPT-5.2在xhigh推理努力时的表现，而每次试验成本仅约5%。当meta-evaluator出错时，倾向于依赖表面线索而非操作性推理。

Conclusion: 实施draft-verify-revise流水线的上下文工程师应警惕指示性漂移，并在每个阶段显式明确预期的指代对象，以避免跨阶段上下文级联引发的所指不一致问题。

Abstract: Draft-verify-revise is a common LLM orchestration pattern for scaling inference-time compute. One LLM drafts, a second critiques the draft and provides feedback, and a third uses that feedback to revise the draft into the final output. As context cascades between stages, LLMs at different stages can resolve a context-dependent expression such as "previous" differently. When that happens, the expression undergoes a deictic shift, a change in what it refers to. This phenomenon was studied with a synthetic dataset of 10 base examples, each rendered in three conditions. Holding the shared components constant, the conditions varied whether the draft stage LLM (the assistant) or the verify stage LLM (the grader) resolved the expression correctly, and how much independent reasoning the revise stage LLM (the meta-evaluator) needed to determine which reading was correct. Six models from three providers were tested across 21 reasoning effort configurations using e-values for sequential testing, in a primary experiment and an ablation experiment that removed error classification labels from the grader's feedback. A separate LLM analyzed the meta-evaluator's stated rationale for each wrong verdict. Balanced accuracy (the unweighted mean of sensitivity and specificity) ranged from 0.156, below chance, to near-perfect. GPT-5.2 rose from 0.156 without reasoning to 0.942 at its highest reasoning effort level, while Gemini 3 Pro stayed above 0.94 at every level. Gemini 3 Pro at low reasoning effort outscored GPT-5.2 at xhigh reasoning effort for roughly 5% of the cost per trial. When the meta-evaluator erred, it tended to rely on surface cues rather than operational reasoning. Context engineers implementing draft-verify-revise pipelines should be wary of deictic shifts and make the intended referent explicit at each stage.

</details>


### [141] [GLARE: Generative Learning via Adversarial Reward Estimation For Social Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12165)
*Tenghao Huang,Zhaoxuan Tan,Muhao Chen,Jonathan May,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出会议动态预测基准MDFB（2207场会议、24794条未来导向问题），并给出基于对抗模仿学习的GLARE方法，在效用与人类相似度上超越SFT和SPIN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议延续需要跟踪议程、发言角色、参与者意图与分歧，但缺乏对长时多方讨论进行未来预测与评估的基准和方法。

Method: 构建MDFB：给定转录前缀和当前问题，模型一次生成合理的多轮延续；评估效用（问题推进）与人类相似度（对话流畅性与角色一致性），不要求复现真实未来。提出GLARE，将对抗模仿学习适配到条件语言生成：判别器将观测延续排在当前actor采样之上，其分数产生KL正则化的策略奖励，并用当前策略负样本重训以演化奖励景观。

Result: GLARE在人类评估中效用平均胜率0.66、人类相似度0.70，优于SFT与SPIN，但仍低于真实人类延续。MDFB亦可作为社会推理竞技场，通过参考辅助判断比较通用模型（含闭源系统）。

Conclusion: MDFB兼具任务专用学习与基于输出的会议行为评估价值。

Abstract: Meeting continuation requires tracking the agenda, speaker roles, participant intentions, and disagreement across long multi-party discussions. We introduce the Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark (MDFB), constructed from 2,207 real-world meetings and 24,794 future-facing queries. Given a transcript prefix and an active question, a model generates a plausible multi-turn continuation in one call. We evaluate utility---progress toward the question---and human-likeness---plausible conversational flow and role consistency---without requiring exact reproduction of the observed future. We further present GLARE, an adaptation of adversarial imitation learning to conditional language generation. A discriminator ranks the observed continuation above samples from the current actor, and its score supplies a KL-regularized policy reward; retraining on current-policy negatives allows the reward landscape to evolve with the actor. GLARE attains average human-evaluated win rates of 0.66 on utility and 0.70 on human-likeness, outperforming SFT and SPIN while remaining below the observed human continuation.
